Extensive experiments show that the proposed method achieves better generalization performance than existing approaches across multiple unseen target domains.

</details>


### [181] [PhysScene: A Scene Graph Dataset for Scientific Visual Reasoning in Physics Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09368)
*Minghao Zou,Qingtian Zeng,Shangkun Liu,Yanda Meng,Guanghui Yue,Baoquan Zhao,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PhysScene，首个面向物理实验场景的场景图数据集，包含专业仪器、结构化实验装置及功能关系，旨在推动科学视觉推理的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景图数据集主要关注通用自然场景，缺乏针对领域特定和功能导向场景（如科学实验场景）的评估基准，限制了智能监控和分析等应用的发展。

Method: 构建PhysScene数据集，聚焦物理实验场景中的专业仪器、结构化装置和功能关系，强调强语义约束和高关系密度，而非追求大规模数据量。

Result: PhysScene为现有场景解析算法带来新挑战，并填补了现有基准的空白，为科学视觉推理提供了有价值的测试平台。

Conclusion: PhysScene通过聚焦实验场景中的逻辑依赖关系而非空间共现，拓展了场景图在科学领域的应用，有望推动相关算法和应用的进步。

Abstract: Scene Graphs (SGs) provide structured representations of visual scenes by modeling objects and their pairwise relationships. Despite recent progress, existing datasets primarily focus on generic natural contexts, leaving domain-specific and function-oriented scenes largely underexplored. This limitation restricts the evaluation of relational reasoning in scientific experimental scenes, thereby hindering the development of intelligent monitoring, analysis, and related applications in such scenes. To address this gap, we introduce PhysScene, the first SG dataset tailored to physics experiments. PhysScene encompasses specialized instruments, structured experimental setups, and functional relations intrinsic to experimental environments, enabling reasoning that extends beyond spatial co-occurrence to logical dependencies. Rather than pursuing large data scale, PhysScene focuses on strong semantic constraints and high relation density in experimental scenes, posing new challenges for existing scene parsing algorithms while offering opportunities for further improvements. Extensive analyses and experiments show that PhysScene complements existing benchmarks and establishes a valuable testbed for advancing scientific visual reasoning. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/ZMH-SDUST/PhysScene.

</details>


### [182] [Echo-DM: Ultrasound Marker Removal via Conditional Latent Diffusion and Region-Aware Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09378)
*Zhiwei Wang,Tao Huang,Wentao Jiang,Muyi Li,Jianxin Liu,Jian Chen,Jie Zou,Yong Luo,Bo Du,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Echo-DM是一个基于条件潜扩散和区域感知融合的超声图像标记去除框架，采用编码器-扩散-解码器架构，在Echo-PAIR数据集上实现了优于两阶段基线方法的标记去除效果和解剖保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床超声图像中的人工标记（如测量卡尺和文本）会引入捷径偏差，导致深度学习模型依赖标记相关线索而非临床有意义的解剖结构进行下游分析。现有标记去除方法存在依赖掩码易受错误传播、或无掩码确定性修复会过度平滑超声纹理并扰动未受影响背景区域的问题。

Method: Echo-DM遵循编码器-扩散-解码器架构：基于DiT的条件潜扩散网络进行全局修复，区域感知融合模块在端到端无掩码推理下执行保持感知的图像空间细化。基于此核心设计，分别实例化了Echo-DM-V（基于VAE）和Echo-DM-R（基于RAE）两种潜模块变体。

Result: 在Echo-PAIR大规模配对临床超声数据集上的实验表明，Echo-DM在标记去除和解剖保真度方面优于代表性两阶段基线方法，并在不同部署场景下提供了良好的质量-效率权衡。

Conclusion: Echo-DM框架有效解决了超声图像标记去除问题，其架构兼容多种潜模块实例化方式，且数据和代码将开源发布。

Abstract: Clinical ultrasound images often contain artificial markers, such as measurement calipers and text, to assist diagnostic interpretation and comparison. However, these markers can introduce shortcut bias in downstream automated analysis, encouraging deep learning models to rely on marker-related cues rather than clinically meaningful anatomy. Existing marker removal methods are either mask-dependent and vulnerable to error propagation, or mask-free deterministic restorers that may over-smooth ultrasound texture and perturb unaffected background regions. To address these challenges, we present Echo-DM, a framework for ultrasound marker removal via conditional latent diffusion and region-aware fusion. Echo-DM follows a common encoder-diffusion-decoder pipeline, where a DiT-based conditional latent diffusion network performs global restoration and a region-aware fusion module enforces preservation-aware image-space refinement under end-to-end mask-free inference. Building on this fixed core design, we further instantiate Echo-DM-V and Echo-DM-R with VAE-based and RAE-based latent modules, respectively, which demonstrates that the Echo-DM architecture is compatible with diverse latent-module instantiations. Extensive experiments on Echo-PAIR, a large-scale paired clinical ultrasound dataset, demonstrate superior marker removal and strong anatomical fidelity compared with representative two-stage baselines, while providing favorable quality--efficiency trade-offs across deployment settings. Data, code and models will be released at https://github.com/MiliLab/Echo-DM.

</details>


### [183] [An Opticalmechanics Framework for Dynamic Estimation of Multibody Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09383)
*Banglei Guan,Xuanyu Bai,Qingquan Chen,Zibin Liu,Dongcai Tan,Zhenbao Yu,Yang Shang,Qifeng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向多体系统的光力学运动-动力学一体化估计框架，通过图像测量运动学量作为非接触输入，结合遗传算法优化识别未知关节力矩，在气浮平台实验中腕关节力矩估计平均绝对误差0.46 Nm，角速度前向预测误差0.006 rad/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人体动力学分析依赖接触式力/力矩传感器和受控实验室环境，应用受限。本文旨在探索无需接触传感器即可进行动力学估计的方法。

Method: 建立约束多体系统动力学模型，以图像测量的运动学量为非接触输入；通过遗传算法优化最小化模型预测与图像测量运动学量之间的差异，从而识别未知关节力矩。

Result: 气浮平台实验：图像估计的腕关节力矩与传感器测量值相比，平均绝对误差为0.46 Nm；前向预测中，模型预测的角速度与图像测量结果相比，平均绝对误差为0.006 rad/s。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了图像测量与力学建模结合用于非接触动力学估计的可行性，尤其适用于难以直接测量力和力矩的场景。

Abstract: Conventional dynamics analysis of the human body is often constrained by the need for contact force and torque sensors and controlled laboratory environments. To address this issue, this study proposes an opticalmechanics kinematic-dynamic integrated estimation framework for multibody systems. Specifically, a constrained multibody model is established to describe the system dynamics, while image-measured kinematic quantities are used as non contact inputs for dynamic estimation. The unknown joint torque is then identified through a genetic-algorithm based optimization by minimizing the discrepancy between model-predicted and image-measured kinematic quan tities. Experimental validation on an air-bearing platform showed that the wrist joint torque estimated from image data achieved a mean absolute error of 0.46 Nm compared with sensor measurements. In the forward prediction test, the model-predicted angular velocity achieved a mean absolute error of 0.006 rad/s relative to the image-measured results. This study demonstrates the potential of combining image measurement and mechanical modeling for non-contact dynamic estimation in scenarios where direct force and torque measurement is difficult.

</details>


### [184] [Real-time body pose non-verbal communication with a consistency-based reliability measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09390)
*Alina Marcu,Dragos Costea,Cristina Lazar,Marius Leordeanu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了仅从2D人体姿态识别交流意图，发布了包含十种交流意图的真实全身姿态数据集，在嵌入式GPU上对多种模型进行了基准测试，并证明了模型的自回归自一致性可作为无监督的可靠性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在远程场景（如救援任务）中，身体运动是可靠的交流信号，但现有资源未能分离这一信号：情感语料库混杂了身体、面部、语音和文本，而骨架动作识别基准只标注动作而非传达的信息。需要专门的数据集和模型来支持机器人上实时、低成本的远距离人机通信。

Method: 发布真实全身姿态数据集（含十种交流意图），并与多种合成数据集（IPC、MotionLCM、VEO3.1、Kimodo）对比。在嵌入式GPU（NVIDIA Orin Nano）上对从骨架图分类器到联合运动预测网络的多类模型进行基准测试，同时评估准确率和帧率。提出模型的自回归自一致性作为无监督可靠性信号，并给出数学证明。

Result: 提供了多种模型在嵌入式GPU上的性能指标（准确率与帧率）。证明了自一致性预测正确的概率随一致步数增加而增长，并给出了自一致性高但预测仍可能错误的条件。

Conclusion: 身体姿态本身足以识别交流意图，在资源受限的机器人平台上可实现实时处理；自回归自一致性是一种有效的无监督可靠性评估方法。

Abstract: Body movement communicates intent at distances and in conditions where neither the face, nor speech can be captured. We study the recognition of communicative intent from 2D body pose alone. We argue that body motion is a reliable signal especially in scenarios that require real time low-cost on-device person-to-robot communication in long distance environments, such as rescue missions. However, existing resources do not isolate this signal. Affective corpora combine body, face, voice and text, while skeleton action-recognition benchmarks label the action performed rather than the message conveyed. We release a dataset of real frames of full-body pose covering ten communicative intents and we compare it against other real (IPC) and synthetic (MotionLCM, VEO3.1, Kimodo) ones that span a range of difficulty. We target systems that can run on a robot's limited onboard hardware. We benchmark multiple models, from skeleton graph classifiers to joint motion-forecasting networks, and report performance metrics together with frame rate on an embedded GPU (NVIDIA Orin~Nano), since speed matters as much as accuracy in our scenario. Finally, we show that a model's own autoregressive self-consistency works as an unsupervised reliability signal. We give a short proof that bounds the probability that a self-consistent prediction is correct, show that this probability grows with the number of consistent steps, and identify the conditions under which a confident prediction can still be false, benchmarked against industry-standard metrics.

</details>


### [185] [CapRL++: Unified Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards for Dense Image and Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09393)
*Penghui Yang,Long Xing,Xiaoyi Dong,Yuhang Zang,Yuhang Cao,Yibin Wang,Yujie Zhou,Jiazi Bu,Jianze Liang,Qidong Huang,Jiaqi Wang,Feng Wu,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Captioning Reinforcement Learning++ (CapRL++)，一种基于可验证奖励的强化学习框架，用于多模态标题生成，通过让非视觉语言模型基于生成的标题回答多项选择题来评估标题质量，从而摆脱对人工标注的依赖。在20多个图像和视频基准上评估表明，CapRL++能提升密集标题质量，且预训练后可获得显著的下游增益，紧凑模型性能可媲美Qwen2.5-VL-72B等大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于监督微调（SFT）的标题生成模型依赖昂贵且不可扩展的人工标注，容易导致模型记忆特定答案，缺乏泛化能力和描述多样性。为克服这些局限，作者提出将RLVR应用于多模态标题生成这一开放式任务。

Method: 提出CapRL++框架，采用解耦的两阶段流水线：LVLM生成标题，然后由一个脱离视觉输入的LLM仅基于该标题回答多项选择题，根据其回答准确率计算奖励信号。该奖励是可验证的、无需参考标注的。

Result: 在20多个图像和视频基准上，CapRL++提升了密集标题质量；预训练后可获得显著的下游任务增益；紧凑模型在Prism框架下达到与Qwen2.5-VL-72B和Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B等大模型相当的密集标题性能。

Conclusion: CapRL++有效训练模型生成可泛化、高保真的描述，为突破传统SFT的局限性建立了稳健的基础。

Abstract: Image and video captioning are fundamental tasks that bridge the visual and linguistic domains, playing a critical role in pre-training Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). Current state-of-the-art captioning models are typically trained with Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), a paradigm that relies on expensive, non-scalable annotations and often causes models to memorize specific ground-truth answers, limiting their generality and ability to generate diverse, creative descriptions. To overcome these limitations, we propose applying Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) to the open-ended task of multimodal captioning. We introduce Captioning Reinforcement Learning++ (CapRL++), a novel reference-free training framework that redefines caption quality through its utility: a high-quality caption should enable a non-visual language model to accurately answer questions about the corresponding visual content. CapRL++ employs a decoupled two-stage pipeline where an LVLM generates a caption, and the objective reward is derived from the accuracy of a separate, vision-free LLM answering Multiple-Choice Questions based solely on that caption. Evaluations on more than 20 image and video benchmarks show that CapRL++ improves dense caption quality and strengthens caption-based pretraining across tasks such as spatial and temporal understanding. Pretraining on scalable image and video caption datasets annotated by CapRL++ yields substantial downstream gains. Furthermore, within the Prism Framework for caption quality evaluation, compact models trained with CapRL++ achieve dense captioning performance comparable to substantially larger models such as Qwen2.5-VL-72B and Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B. These results validate that CapRL++ effectively trains models to produce generalizable, high-fidelity descriptions, establishing a robust foundation beyond the limitations of traditional SFT.

</details>


### [186] [vesselFM-CT: Segmenting All Blood Vessels in CT Images for System-Level Cardiovascular Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09400)
*Bastian Wittmann,Chinmay Prabhakar,Suprosanna Shit,Bjoern Menze*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出vesselFM-CT，首个能够在3D CT图像中鲁棒分割所有血管（从大血管到微小肠系膜血管）的模型，通过迭代多步训练和TubeLoss损失函数解决心血管系统的异质性挑战，性能优于所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多聚焦于血管网络的孤立片段，无法评估整个心血管系统的整体健康状态和功能完整性。为弥合这一差距，实现从最大到最微小血管的全方位分割。

Method: 提出vesselFM-CT模型，采用迭代多步训练策略，并设计了TubeLoss损失函数，以有效应对心血管系统固有的异质性（血管半径、长度、拓扑结构、分叉模式的巨大差异）。

Result: vesselFM-CT在所有基线方法中表现最优，实现了CT图像中心血管系统的自动化精确提取。

Conclusion: 该模型解锁了广泛的临床和技术应用前景，包括自动化疾病分类和合成CT图像生成，有助于推进临床诊断和血管生理学基础理解。

Abstract: The vascular network in the human body is characterized by blood vessels exhibiting drastic structural variations in radius, length, topological properties, and branching patterns. This heterogeneity, together with location-specific anatomical background variations, poses a significant challenge for robust, large-scale analysis of the entire cardiovascular system. As a result, most research has focused on narrow, isolated segments of the vascular network. While such targeted studies provide valuable insights, they inherently limit the ability to assess the systemic health and functional integrity of the vascular network as a whole. In this work, we aim to bridge this gap to advance both clinical diagnostics and our fundamental understanding of vascular physiology. We propose the task of segmenting all vessels in CT images, ranging from the largest components of the cardiovascular system to even minuscule mesenteric vessels. To this end, we introduce vesselFM-CT, the first model capable of robustly segmenting all blood vessels in 3D CT images. VesselFM-CT is trained via an iterative, multi-step process and optimizes our proposed TubeLoss loss function, effectively addressing the inherent heterogeneity of the cardiovascular system. We demonstrate that vesselFM-CT outperforms all baselines and enables automated, precise extraction of the cardiovascular system from CT images, thereby unlocking a wide range of clinical and technical perspectives, including automated disease classification and synthetic CT image generation.

</details>


### [187] [Leveraging Morphology for Historical Script Metrological Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09446)
*Malamatenia Vlachou Efstathiou,Raphaël Baena,Dominique Stutzmann,Mathieu Aubry*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Transformer和原型学习的形态学手写文本分析方法，仅需行级转录监督即可学习字符原型，实现可扩展、有意义的古文字学测量，并在14世纪手稿上验证了其区分书写者特征的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手写文本识别虽能大规模转录历史文档，但无法为古文字学研究提供可解释的视觉测量指标；传统方法需要字符级标注，成本高昂且难以规模化。

Method: 采用基于Transformer的检测架构配合原型行重建模块，从行级转录中学习字符原型及其出现频率、形变和位置信息；仅需单列文本数据即可进行测量。

Result: 在160页14世纪手稿（BnF fr. 2813）上验证，该方法不仅能区分不同书写者的图形特征，还能发现和分析细微变化；显著优于Learnable Typewriter基线，实现了精确的字符边界框预测。

Conclusion: 该方法为古文字学提供了可扩展、节俭（数据需求低）的自动测量工具，可应用于字符、双字母组及图形单元间距分析，具有重要的古文字学价值。

Abstract: Advances in handwritten text recognition have enabled large-scale transcription of historical documents, but still provide limited access to interpretable visual measurements for paleography, the study of historical scripts. In this paper, our main insight is that morphological script analysis, in particular the capacity to learn character prototypes from line-level transcriptions, enables the definition of scalable, meaningful, and stable paleographic measurements. More precisely, we leverage a transformer-based detection architecture together with a prototype-based line reconstruction module to learn prototypical characters and their occurrence, deformation, and positioning.
  Our contributions are twofold. First, we introduce a deep architecture and learning methodology that enables efficient character modeling with only line-level transcription supervision, significantly improving over the Learnable Typewriter baseline and enabling accurate character bounding box prediction, unlocking its potential for paleographic measurements. Second, we introduce and demonstrate the paleographical relevance of automatic measurements enabled by our architecture for characters, bi-grams, and spaces between graphical units. For this demonstration, we extend the annotations of the codex Paris, BnF, fr. 2813, commissioned in the late fourteenth century by Charles V and copied by four hands, to 160 pages. We visualize our measurements over these pages, showing how they enable us not only to differentiate graphical profiles, but also to discover and analyze subtle variations. This case study outlines the scalability of our approach and its frugality in terms of required training data, since a single column of text is sufficient to compute our measurements on each of the 160 pages.
  Data and code are publicly available at: https://malamatenia.github.io/morphology4metrology-analysis.

</details>


### [188] [GD-MIL: Grade-Disentangled Multiple Instance Learning for Multimodal Biochemical Recurrence Prediction in Prostate Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09453)
*Dasari Naga Raju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过严格的基准测试发现，H&E病理切片在生化复发预测中存在超越Gleason分级的预后信息，并提出了Grade-Disentangled MIL (GD-MIL)框架，通过对抗学习去除Gleason分级影响后，与临床变量融合实现了C-index 0.704，显著优于临床基线（0.687）和纯影像模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前列腺癌生化复发风险分层几乎完全依赖Gleason分级主导的临床变量，而H&E病理切片中是否存在超越分级的预后信号，以及多实例学习能否恢复这一信号，仍是一个未解决的问题。同时，许多现有流程因在评估折上选择模型检查点而人为膨胀了模型一致性。

Method: 构建了TCGA-PRAD上487名患者、101个BCR事件的严格基准，使用五折交叉验证（五个种子）加严格折外评分。对比了不同MIL聚合器（ABMIL, CLAM, TransMIL, PatchGCN）和特征提取器（ResNet50 vs 病理基础模型）。提出了GD-MIL——一种带梯度反转分级对抗的门控注意力MIL编码器，在晚期融合临床变量之前，通过对抗学习使切片表示对Gleason分级不敏感。

Result: MIL聚合器影响很小（C-index 0.61-0.64），特征提取器是主导因素（ResNet50 0.566 vs 病理基础模型0.639）。临床Cox模型达0.687，无纯影像模型显著优于它。GD-MIL达C-index 0.704，显著优于临床基线（Δ=+0.029, p=0.0005）和最优纯影像模型（Δ=+0.062, p=0.039），中位风险分层log-rank p<0.0001（5年BCR-free ~20% vs ~70%）。

Conclusion: H&E形态学包含超越Gleason分级的互补预后信息；GD-MIL通过解耦分级信号实现了对临床变量的显著提升，为前列腺癌术后生化复发风险预测提供了新的病理影像-临床融合框架。

Abstract: Biochemical recurrence (BCR) after radical prostatectomy is a critical endpoint in prostate cancer, yet risk stratification relies almost entirely on variables dominated by Gleason grade. Whether H&E whole slide images (WSIs) carry prognostic signal beyond grade, and whether multiple instance learning (MIL) can recover it, remains unsettled. A key obstacle is that many pipelines select model checkpoints on the evaluation fold, artificially inflating concordance. We construct a rigorous benchmark on TCGA-PRAD (487 patients, 101 BCR events) using strict out-of-fold scoring over five-fold cross-validation repeated across five seeds. The choice of MIL aggregator (ABMIL, CLAM, TransMIL, PatchGCN) has little effect (C-index 0.61-0.64 with UNI2-h), while the feature extractor is the dominant factor (ResNet50 0.566 versus pathology foundation models up to 0.639). A clinical Cox model on grade, stage, and age reaches 0.687; no imaging-only model significantly outperforms it (p > 0.10). We introduce Grade-Disentangled MIL (GD-MIL), a gated-attention MIL encoder trained with a gradient-reversal grade adversary that encourages the slide representation to be invariant to Gleason grade before late fusion with clinical variables. GD-MIL achieves C-index 0.704, significantly outperforming both the clinical baseline (delta-c = +0.029, p = 0.0005) and the best imaging-only model (delta-c = +0.062, p = 0.039), suggesting H&E morphology contains prognostic information complementary to grade. A median risk split yields log-rank p < 0.0001 separation in BCR-free survival (~20% vs ~70% at five years).

</details>


### [189] [Training-Free Generalized Few-Shot Segmentation through Open-Vocabulary Semantic Arbitration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09474)
*Silas Kwabla Gah,Ebenezer Owusu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Open-V是一种无需训练的通用小样本语义分割框架，通过推理时协调冻结的语义先验（SAM+CLIP），在多个基准上超越了有训练的基线方法，并揭示了基础模型分割中的可重复性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GFSS方法需要任务特定的参数适应来学习新类别，而近期基础模型已具备强大的开放词汇识别能力。作者希望探索是否可以通过推理时协调冻结的语义先验（而非参数适应）来解决GFSS问题。

Method: 提出Open-V框架，结合SAM的Promptable Concept Segmentation (PCS)与K-shot CLIP支持质心，通过逐像素校准的语义仲裁机制进行推理。无任何可训练组件，支持任意语义类别。

Result: 在PASCAL-5i（1-shot）上，基类/新类/调和mIoU分别达到78.4/77.5/77.9，无需GFSS特定训练即超越最强有训练基线+17.7 HM。在PASCAL-5i、COCO-20i、ADE-OW上均验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 支持信息可通过推理时语义 grounding 融入，且在基础模型文本先验较弱时贡献更大；发现基础模型分割中存在因预处理和评估空间不匹配导致的可重复性问题；训练无关的基础模型先验协调在传统GFSS和开放词汇评估中均能泛化。

Abstract: Generalized Few-Shot Semantic Segmentation (GFSS) has traditionally been approached as a representation-learning problem, requiring task-specific adaptation to incorporate novel classes from limited support examples. Recent foundation models, however, already exhibit strong open-vocabulary recognition and segmentation capabilities, raising a different question: can GFSS be solved through inference-time coordination of frozen semantic priors rather than parameter adaptation? We answer this question with Open-V, a training-free GFSS framework that combines Segment Anything (SAM3) Promptable Concept Segmentation (PCS) with a K-shot CLIP support centroid through calibrated per-pixel semantic arbitration. OpenV introduces no trainable components and supports arbitrary semantic categories at inference time. Beyond segmentation performance, our study contributes three broader findings. First, we show that support information can be incorporated through inference-time semantic grounding, and that its contribution increases as foundation-model text priors weaken on label-disjoint vocabularies. Second, we identify a reproducibility confound in foundationmodel segmentation, demonstrating that preprocessing and evaluation-space mismatches can silently distort reported performance. Finally, we validate Open-V across PASCAL5i, COCO-20i, and ADE-OW, showing that training-free coordination of foundation-model priors generalizes across both conventional GFSS and open-vocabulary evaluation settings. On PASCAL-5i (1-shot), Open-V attains base/novel/harmonic mIoU of 78.4/77.5/77.9, without GFSS-specific training surpassing the strongest trained baseline by +17.7 HM.

</details>


### [190] [Efficient Minimal Solvers for Visual-Inertial Relative Pose Estimation in Multi-Camera Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09477)
*Tao Li,Zhenbao Yu,Banglei Guan,Jianli Han,Weimin Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种高效的最小求解器，通过新型参数化方法估计多相机系统的相对位姿。第一种利用IMU提供的垂直方向先验，第二种利用旋转轴方向先验。两种方法仅需4个点对应，将多相机相对位姿估计问题简化为求解一元六次多项式（现有方法通常为八次多项式），在RANSAC框架中展现出显著效率优势，在KITTI基准测试中实现了优越的计算效率和有竞争力的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多相机系统相对位姿估计方法存在计算复杂度高或需要过多点对应的问题，限制了实际应用。本文旨在通过引入IMU先验信息和新参数化方法，降低计算复杂度和对应点数量需求。

Method: 提出两种基于新型参数化的高效最小求解器：1) 利用IMU垂直方向先验的求解器；2) 利用IMU旋转轴方向先验的求解器。两种方法仅需4个点对应，将问题简化为求解一元六次多项式方程。

Result: 在合成数据和KITTI基准测试中，所提方法在计算效率上优于现有算法，精度具有竞争力。将多项式次数从现有方法的8次降至6次，点对应需求减少。

Conclusion: 提出的两种求解器显著降低了多相机相对位姿估计的计算复杂度和对应点需求，特别适合集成到RANSAC框架中，在视觉里程计等应用中具有良好潜力。

Abstract: Estimating the relative poses of multi-camera systems is a fundamental problem in computer vision, with critical applications in autonomous vehicles, mobile devices, and unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). However, existing solutions often suffer from high computational complexity or rely on an excessive number of point correspondences, limiting their real-world applicability. To address these limitations, we propose two efficient minimal solvers for estimating the relative poses of multi-camera systems using a novel parameterization. The first solver leverages the vertical direction prior provided by Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs), while the second utilizes the rotation axis direction prior from IMUs. Our methods require only four point correspondences and reduce the problem of multi-camera relative pose estimation to solving a univariate 6th-degree polynomial, a significant improvement over existing approaches, which typically involve 8th-degree polynomials. This reduction in computational complexity and correspondence requirements makes our solvers particularly effective when integrated into RANSAC frameworks, demonstrating strong potential for visual odometry applications. Through rigorous evaluations on synthetic data and the KITTI benchmark, our methods achieved superior computational efficiency and competitive accuracy compared to state-of-the-art algorithms.

</details>


### [191] [Optical Music Recognition for Real-World Manuscripts with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09479)
*Jiří Mayer,Martina Dvořáková,Vojtěch Dvořák,Markéta Herzánová Vlková,Filip Bím,Pavel Pecina,Samuel Šomorjai,Petr Žabička,Jan Hajič*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了资源受限场景下真实手稿乐谱的光学音乐识别（OMR）问题，发现现有基于合成数据的方法虽然有效，但领域内真实标注数据仍不可或缺；同时，细粒度符号标注无需领域内样本，可利用合成数据替代，从而推动OMR在文化遗产保护中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OMR方法在数字乐谱上表现优异，但在真实手稿（尤其是复杂钢琴谱）上严重失效，而文化遗产机构资源有限，无法大规模标注领域内数据，亟需探索在资源受限条件下实现OMR的方法。

Method: 使用MuNG细粒度乐谱图标注和Smashcima合成工具，在真实手稿数据集上建立基线；通过对比直接转录、领域内数据标注与合成数据域自适应等方法，评估不同策略对OMR性能的影响。

Result: 直接转录领域内真实数据仍然必不可少；域自适应（使用合成手稿图像）能显著提升性能；细粒度符号标注无需领域内样本，合成数据即可替代。

Conclusion: 为资源受限场景下的真实手稿OMR提供了首个基线，表明合成数据可降低对昂贵细粒度标注的依赖，但仍需少量领域内真实标注，推动OMR向文化遗产保护目标迈进。

Abstract: Optical Music Recognition (OMR) has seen major progress in model design, with end-to-end methods now capable of recognising notation at all levels of complexity. However, the impact of this progress has been limited by the visual domains of available training datasets, which are largely born-digital. Existing large collections of sheet music in libraries and other heritage institutions contain predominantly manuscripts, whose visual domains are highly diverse and different, so existing OMR systems fail when applied in the real world. These institutions are often resource-constrained, so large in-domain datasets cannot be expected. We provide a first baseline on real-world manuscripts with complex piano notation in the resource-constrained scenario. Using fine-grained music notation graph (MuNG) annotations and the Smashcima synthesis tool, we then show that while some direct transcriptions of in-domain data remain essential, domain adaptation using synthetic musical manuscript images brings significant improvement. Furthermore, the symbols used do not need to be in-domain, so the expensive fine-grained annotation can be avoided. We thus bring OMR closer to one of its stated goals: preserving and promoting musical cultural heritage.

</details>


### [192] [ContextShift: A Controlled Benchmark for Context Dependence in Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09495)
*Dan Zlotnikov,Alex Lazarovich,Ohad Ben-Shahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ContextShift是一个系统性地操控物体-上下文关系的基准测试，揭示了目标检测器在上下文变化时出现预测抑制现象（假阴性增加高达227%，预测量减少达44%），而AP等聚合指标无法捕捉这种退化。性能沿统计共现轴呈非单调变化，且上下文增强训练可部分恢复鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估依赖AP等聚合指标，无法揭示目标检测器在上下文变化下的性能退化模式。

Method: 构建ContextShift基准，通过几何变换及合成/自然背景替换系统操控物体-上下文关系，并基于NPMI定义连续兼容性轴，在多种检测器架构上进行评估。

Result: 假阴性增加高达227%，预测量减少达44%，假阳性稳定或下降；AP无法捕捉退化；退化主要由有效检测候选减少而非置信度下降驱动；性能沿NPMI轴呈非单调（中间峰值）；上下文增强可部分恢复性能。

Conclusion: 上下文变化会导致检测器的预测抑制退化，AP等聚合指标具有误导性，而上下文感知增强训练可提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: Modern object detectors achieve strong performance on standard benchmarks, yet their robustness to contextual variation remains insufficiently understood. Prior evaluations largely rely on aggregate metrics such as AP on uncontrolled distribution shifts, which can obscure how performance degrades under context change. We introduce ContextShift, a controlled benchmark that systematically manipulates object--context relationships while preserving object appearance. Built on COCO 2017, it isolates context as an independent variable through geometric transformations and synthetic and natural background substitutions, including a continuous compatibility axis based on normalized pointwise mutual information (NPMI). Across diverse detector architectures, we observe a consistent degradation pattern: false negatives increase by up to 227% and prediction volume decreases by up to 44%, while false positives remain stable or decline. This suppression behavior is not captured by aggregate metrics such as AP, which can mask substantial recall loss and changes in prediction dynamics. Further analysis suggests that degradation is driven less by reduced confidence than by a reduced formation of valid detection candidates. Moreover, performance along the statistical compatibility axis is non-monotonic, peaking at intermediate NPMI and degrading toward both extremes, indicating that statistical co-occurrence does not correlate linearly with effective visual context. Finally, we show that context-aware augmentation improves robustness: every augmented variant outperforms the dataset-only baseline on both original and manipulated test images, partially recovering performance lost to prediction-suppression failures by exposing models to object--context decoupling during training.

</details>


### [193] [Prisma-World: Camera-Controllable Multi-Agent Video World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09507)
*Huiqiang Sun,Zhan Peng,Size Wu,Kun Wang,Kang Liao,Dianyi Wang,Xingyu Zeng,Sheng Jin,Yangguang Li,Zhiguo Cao,Ziwei Liu,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Prisma-World，一种摄像机可控的多智能体世界模型，通过联合几何感知去噪过程实现跨视图一致性，并配套构建了大规模UE5数据集PrismaDataset用于训练和评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型大多基于单一观察者，扩展到多智能体时，独立生成各智能体未来状态会导致重叠视图间出现不一致的物体、布局和外观，传统摄像机条件控制无法显式耦合共享场景几何下的视图生成。

Method: 提出Prisma-World，将所有智能体视频放入全注意力序列中处理；设计多智能体RoPE区分身份并保持同步时间坐标；在注意力中注入相对摄像机几何以偏置重叠视角共享场景证据；采用重叠衰减课程训练范式和小地图条件结构引导增强多视图一致性与全局空间感知。同时构建PrismaDataset（UE5大规模数据集），支持全景采集、可组合多智能体视图组及精确标注。

Result: 单个Prisma-World模型能够生成高保真多智能体视频，支持灵活数量的智能体、摄像机可控、跨视图一致性提升以及在小地图引导下的空间接地。

Conclusion: 多智能体世界模型通过联合几何感知去噪和显式跨视图一致性约束，可以有效解决独立生成中的场景不一致问题。

Abstract: Video world models have made rapid progress in generating controllable visual experiences, but most of them still simulate the world from a single observer. Extending such models to multiple agents raises a central challenge: if each agent's future state is generated independently, overlapping views may instantiate different versions of the same scene, leading to inconsistent objects, layouts, and appearances across agents. Conventional camera conditioning controls individual trajectories, but it does not explicitly couple the generation of views that should agree under shared scene geometry. We introduce Prisma-World, a camera-controllable multi-agent world model that formulates multi-agent generation as a joint geometry-aware denoising process for cross-view consistency. Prisma-World processes all agent videos within one full-attention sequence, uses a multi-agent RoPE design to distinguish agent identities while preserving synchronized temporal coordinates, and injects relative camera geometry into attention to bias overlapping viewpoints toward shared scene evidence. To further strengthen multi-view consistency and enhance global spatial perception, we augment our framework with an overlap-decaying curriculum training paradigm alongside minimap-conditioned structural guidance. To facilitate the training and evaluation of multi-agent models, we introduce PrismaDataset, a large-scale UE5 dataset with panoramic acquisition across diverse scenes, composable multi-agent view groups with flexible agent counts and complex camera trajectories, and precise camera/action annotations for consistency training and evaluation. Experiments show that a single Prisma-World model can generate high-fidelity multi-agent videos with flexible agent numbers, camera controllability, improved cross-view consistency, and spatial grounding under minimap guidance.

</details>


### [194] [Securing Self-supervised Data Curation for Foundation Models Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09511)
*Sandeep Gupta,Roberto Passerone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PDD（中毒数据检测器）的主动防御机制，结合ImageBind模型与传统分类器（RF、KNN、NB、SVM），用于检测自监督学习数据 curation 过程中的中毒数据，确保基础模型训练前数据集的完整性。实验在176,200张图像上验证，SVM-PDD在分布内和分布外场景均表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督数据 curation 虽能降低标注成本并扩展数据集规模，但依赖匿名外部来源增加了数据投毒风险，现有方法缺乏针对SSL curated数据集的有效完整性校验手段。

Method: 利用预训练ImageBind模型提取多模态特征，结合RF、KNN、NB、SVM四种传统分类器构建PDD；采用集成方法实现新攻击检测器的快速扩展；在176,200张图像的三个数据集上针对三种对抗攻击进行评估。

Result: SVM-PDD在分布内（Set3-Set5）和分布外（TrueFace和140K RealFace）场景均达到最优性能，PDD设计具有良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: PDD能有效保障自监督数据集的完整性，且通过集成方法可快速整合新的对抗攻击检测器，具备较强的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Self-supervised data curation provides a pathway to scaling and improving the generalization capabilities of machine learning models. By leveraging self-supervised learning (SSL) for data curation, the demand for massive training datasets required by foundation models can be effectively met. SSL greatly alleviates the costs associated with annotation and manual dataset curation while minimizing the need for human oversight. However, the integrity of SSL-curated datasets must be rigorously checked, as reliance on anonymous and unvetted external sources can substantially increase the risk of data poisoning. In this paper, we propose a Poisoned Data Detector (PDD), an active defense mechanism designed to ensure the integrity of SSL-curated datasets prior to foundation model training. PDDs are designed using a combination of the pretrained ImageBind model and traditional classifiers, including Random Forest (RF), k-Nearest Neighbors (KNN), Naive Bayes (NB), and Support Vector Machines (SVM). We rigorously evaluated PDDs using 176,200 images from three diverse datasets and three different adversarial attacks encompassing both in-distribution and out-of-distribution scenarios. Notably, SVM-PDD achieves superior performance for both in-distribution (Set3-Set5) and out-of-distribution (TrueFace and 140K RealFace) datasets. Our design demonstrates strong scalability and enables the rapid integration of new adversarial attack detectors through an ensemble approach.

</details>


### [195] [SwiftVR: Real-Time One-Step Generative Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09516)
*Jiaqi Yan,Xiangyu Chen,Xinlin Zhong,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Jie Liu,Jiantao Zhou,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SwiftVR 提出了一种流式单步扩散视频恢复框架，通过无掩码移位窗口自注意力和轻量级恢复感知自编码器，在消费级GPU上实现了首个实时1080p视频流恢复，在单张H100上可达31 FPS（2560×1440）和14 FPS（3840×2160）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单步扩散视频恢复模型在消费级GPU上部署存在两大瓶颈：高分辨率下的二次空间注意力计算以及大视频自编码器带来的延迟-内存开销，导致无法实现实时高清视频流恢复。

Method: 1) 无掩码移位窗口自注意力：通过确定性索引将每个空间窗口聚集成密集张量，在标准密集SDPA路径上执行注意力，无需掩码、循环移位、填充或硬件专用稀疏核；2) 恢复感知轻量级自编码器：支持快速分块解码，同时保持重建质量；3) 因果分块协议下的流式处理框架。

Result: H100上：2560×1440分辨率31 FPS，3840×2160分辨率14 FPS；RTX 5090上：1920×1080分辨率26 FPS。所有对比扩散VR基线在4K下均超出内存限制。SwiftVR是首个在消费级GPU上实现实时1080p流媒体的生成式VR模型。

Conclusion: SwiftVR通过标准密集SDPA和轻量级自编码器设计，成功将单步扩散视频恢复模型部署到消费级GPU并实现实时1080p性能，且具有较强的无参考感知质量和较低的推理成本。

Abstract: Real-time video restoration (VR) for live streams requires high-resolution outputs under strict per-frame latency constraints. Existing one-step diffusion-based VR models remain difficult to deploy on consumer-grade GPUs due to two main bottlenecks: quadratic spatial attention at high resolutions and the latency-memory overhead of large video autoencoders. We present SwiftVR, a streaming one-step generative VR framework that reduces both bottlenecks under a causal chunk-wise protocol. For attention, mask-free shifted-window self-attention gathers each spatial window into a dense tensor via deterministic indexing, keeping all attention calls on the dense scaled dot-product attention path without masks, cyclic shifts, padding, or hardware-specific sparse kernels. Because SwiftVR uses only standard dense SDPA calls, the trained model transfers to consumer GPUs without retraining or custom kernels. For autoencoding, a lightweight Restoration-aware Autoencoder enables fast chunk-wise decoding while preserving reconstruction quality. On a single H100, SwiftVR sustains 31~FPS at 2560x1440 and 14~FPS at 3840x2160, whereas all compared diffusion-based VR baselines exceed the memory limit at 4K. On a consumer RTX~5090, SwiftVR reaches 26~FPS at 1920x1080. To our knowledge, SwiftVR is the first generative VR model to achieve real-time 1080p streaming on a consumer-grade GPU, while attaining strong no-reference perceptual quality with lower inference cost. Project is available at https://h-oliday.github.io/SwiftVR.

</details>


### [196] [Adversarial Attack and Disturbance Detection by Hadamard-Coded Output Representations for Object Detection and Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09536)
*Lucas Görnhardt,Timo Bartels,Niklas Schwarz,Tim Fingscheidt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HadamardNet框架，将Hadamard码作为输出表示用于语义分割和目标检测。通过求解概率单纯形投影实现最优解码，利用预测不一致性进行对抗攻击检测，在单次检测中达到SOTA扰动检测性能，同时保持清洁数据的参考性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统独热编码导致模型校准差、攻击下过于自信、基于熵的检测算法失效；Hadamard编码已证明可提升图像分类的鲁棒性，但在语义分割中落后于SOTA，在目标检测中尚未被探索；此外，现有工作未解决码字内在不一致性或利用码字冗余。

Method: 1) 推导新的Hadamard码字解码过程，通过投影到概率单纯形求解最优类概率优化问题；2) 利用该优化结果衡量预测不一致性；3) 将这些不一致性用于对抗攻击和扰动检测；4) 构建HadamardNet框架，将Hadamard码作为语义分割和目标检测的输出表示。

Result: 在语义分割和目标检测任务上，对扰动和对抗攻击均达到SOTA的扰动检测性能，仅需单次检测；在干净数据上达到等同或接近的参考性能。

Conclusion: Hadamard输出编码可有效推广到语义分割和目标检测任务，通过解决码字不一致性和利用冗余性，在保持基础任务性能的同时显著提升对抗鲁棒性。

Abstract: Conventional one-hot encodings often yield poorly calibrated models, being overconfident under attack, and letting entropy-based detection algorithms fail. Previous image classification works have demonstrated that Hadamard-coded output representations can improve adversarial robustness. However, attempts to integrate Hadamard codes into semantic segmentation fall far behind state-of-the-art models in mean intersection-over-union performance. Regarding object detection, such output encodings have not yet been investigated at all. Further, no prior art addressed intrinsic codeword inconsistencies or actually exploited intrinsic codeword redundancy. Accordingly, we first derive a novel decoding procedure for Hadamard codewords towards optimal class-wise probabilities, solving the underlying optimization problem by using the projection onto the probability simplex. Second, our optimization delivers a measure of prediction inconsistency. Third, we are the first to show how to exploit these inconsistencies for adversarial attack and disturbance detection. Fourth, we introduce HadamardNet, a framework employing Hadamard codes as output representations for semantic segmentation and object detection models and tasks. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation both on disturbances and adversarial attacks, achieving state-of-the-art perturbation detection performance for both tasks in only a single detection pass, while delivering equivalent or close-by reference performance on clean data.

</details>


### [197] [A VideoMAE-v2 Approach to Zero-Shot Traffic Accident Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09542)
*Siyuan Li,Xiaoyang Bi,Mengshi Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种零样本交通事故预测框架，使用VideoMAE-v2骨干网络和逐帧预测头，在仅使用公开二分类驾驶事故数据集训练的情况下，泛化到未见过的行车记录仪视频，并在2026年CVPR@AUTOPILOT零样本事故预测竞赛中获得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通事故预测在部署时难以大规模收集带标注的事故视频数据，成本高昂。作者希望在零样本设置下，仅从公开的二分类标注数据中学习，泛化到未见过的行车记录仪场景。

Method: 使用VideoMAE-v2作为骨干网络，结合逐帧预测头，并采用滑动窗口协议来处理帧级时间风险估计任务，利用粗粒度的二分类标注数据训练出能进行细粒度时序预测的模型。

Result: 该方法在2026年CVPR@AUTOPILOT零样本事故预测竞赛中获得第二名。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效弥合了粗粒度二分类标注与帧级时序风险评估之间的差距，证明了在零样本场景下进行交通事故预测的可行性。

Abstract: Traffic accident anticipation -- predicting the likelihood of an imminent collision at every frame of a dashcam video -- is safety-critical yet difficult to scale, because collecting in-domain annotated accident footage for every deployment scenario is prohibitively expensive. We study this task under a zero-shot setting where no target-domain training data is available: the model must learn exclusively from a publicly available binary-labelled driving-accident dataset and generalise to unseen dashcam footage. We propose a framework that bridges the gap between the frame-level temporal risk estimation task and coarsely labelled binary accident datasets by coupling a VideoMAE-v2 backbone with a per-frame prediction head under a sliding-window protocol. Our method achieves 2nd place in the 2026 CVPR@AUTOPILOT Zero-Shot Accident Anticipation competition. Code is available at https://github.com/TimeSouth/zero-shot-taa-solution.

</details>


### [198] [Streaming Interventions: Can Video Large Language Models Correct Mistakes as They Occur?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09547)
*Apratim Bhattacharyya,Shweta Mahajan,Sanjay Haresh,Rajeev Yasarla,Reza Pourreza,Litian Liu,Risheek Garrepalli,Roland Memisevic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于评估视频大语言模型在烹饪场景中主动干预能力的基准Ego-MC-Bench，并针对其训练数据缺乏问题，通过构建反事实合成数据集Ego-CoMist进行微调，显著提升了小模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频大语言模型缺乏在实时任务指导中主动发现用户错误并进行干预的能力，而这一能力对于实际应用至关重要。目前缺乏针对这一能力的评估基准和训练数据。

Method: 1) 构建Ego-MC-Bench基准，评估视频LLM在烹饪场景中逐步任务指导的主动干预能力。2) 通过将非交互式烹饪视频转化为包含主动干预监督样本的反事实合成数据集Ego-CoMist。

Result: 实验表明Ego-MC-Bench对现有最先进的视频LLM极具挑战性；在Ego-CoMist上微调可以提升性能，尤其对适合边缘设备部署的小型高效视频LLM效果显著。

Conclusion: Ego-MC-Bench可作为评估视频LLM主动干预能力的重要基准，Ego-CoMist有效缓解了训练数据匮乏问题，为构建实用的任务指导助手提供了方向。

Abstract: Learning everyday skills, like cooking a dish, relies increasingly on instructional media such as online videos. This opens the door to the use of video (and multimodal) large language models (LLMs) as task guidance assistants. A crucial capability for the real-world success of a prospective task guidance assistant is it's ability to intervene proactively as soon as a mistake is apparent in order to guide the user. To evaluate this crucial capability, we introduce Ego-MC-Bench (Mistake Corrections), a benchmark for evaluating reactive, step-by-step task guidance in realistic cooking scenarios. Extensive experiments show that Ego-MC-Bench is highly challenging for state-of-the-art video LLMs. We argue that a key reason is the limited availability of training data for fine-tuning models on this task. Although there exists a wide range of cooking video datasets, existing datasets lack examples of mistakes along with appropriately timed interventions. To help address this data limitation, we also introduce Ego-CoMist, a counterfactual synthetic dataset created by transforming non -interactive cooking videos into supervised training examples showing proactive interventions. We show that fine-tuning on Ego-CoMist yields performance gains especially for smaller and more efficient video LLMs that are well suited for delivering assistance on edge devices.

</details>


### [199] [TUDSR: Twice Upsampling-Diffusion for Higher Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09608)
*Zhiqiang Wu,Yitong Dong,Xian Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TUDSR提出了一种两阶段上采样扩散框架，通过分阶段训练（先R分辨率训练，再NR分辨率循环块训练）和GAN架构，在SD2.1-base基础上实现了超越原生分辨率限制的高质量图像超分辨率（最高2048²），显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在处理高分辨率（如2048²）超分辨率时质量极差，原因有二：上采样比超出模型原生支持比、模型原生分辨率限制。直接训练原生高分辨率模型需要更大架构，带来巨大的计算开销和GPU显存成本，难以在资源受限设备上实现。

Method: 提出TUDSR框架，包含两阶段训练：第一阶段在R分辨率下训练，第二阶段引入循环块训练策略在NR分辨率下训练。每个阶段采用一步GAN架构（生成器+判别器），基于SD2.1-base构建TUDSR-S。

Result: TUDSR-S在多个基准测试上达到SOTA性能，能够在1024²和2048²分辨率下生成高质量图像，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过两阶段上采样扩散框架，无需训练原生高分辨率大模型即可实现超出模型原生分辨率支持的高质量超分辨率重建，有效解决了高分辨率SR中的计算资源瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have achieved remarkable success in real-world image super-resolution (SR). With tiled diffusion techniques, these models can produce high-resolution images that exceed their native-supported resolution. However, the quality of such high-resolution (e.g $2048^2$) outputs often remains extremely poor, primarily due to two factors we consider: the image upsampling ratio (e.g $\times8$) exceeding the model's native-supported upsampling ratio (e.g $\times4$), and the model's native-supported resolution. In practice, training a native high-resolution model requires larger architectures, which incur significant computational overhead and GPU memory costs, making it hard on limited-resource equipment. Thus, we present TUDSR, a Twice Upsampling-Diffusion framework for higher SR. The TUDSR framework mainly consists of two stages: the first involves training at $R$-resolution, and the second introduces a looped chunk-based training strategy at $NR$-resolution. Each stage adapts a one-step GAN architecture comprising a generator and a discriminator. Based on SD2.1-base, we develop TUDSR-S, which achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks. Extensive experiments further demonstrate that TUDSR-S generates high-quality images at the resolutions of $1024^2$ and even $2048^2$, significantly outperforming existing approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/wuer5/TUDSR.

</details>


### [200] [ATN3D: Density-Aware LiDAR-Radar Early 3D Object Detection Under Extreme Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09634)
*Debojyoti Biswas,Xianbiao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ATN3D，一种面向自动驾驶稀疏远距离场景的激光雷达-雷达融合3D检测框架，通过密度感知融合、占用门控聚合、证据条件通道注意力和距离感知损失，在清晰和雾天条件下显著提升远距离（>30m）检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中远距离（>30m）3D检测面临两个核心挑战：（1）早期多模态融合会丢弃稀疏性信息并引入空/误占网格噪声，降低远距离召回率；（2）上下文无关的均匀通道监督偏向密集近距样本，导致远距小物体优化不足，延迟最早检测时机。

Method: ATN3D包含四个创新模块：(i) 密度感知早期融合——通过跨模态门控根据体素密度/稀疏性和雷达证据调节融合权重；(ii) 占用门控邻域聚合——使用圆形内核仅从可信单元聚合特征；(iii) 证据条件通道自注意力——根据天气和距离自适应调整通道权重；(iv) 距离感知损失——按距离重平衡分类和定位损失，对齐距离分层评估。

Result: 在VoD基准上，清晰天气mAP提升+3.55%，模拟浓雾下提升+8.41%；>30m远距目标上清晰天气+3.33%，浓雾+2.09%。

Conclusion: ATN3D在稀疏感知的道路交通场景下实现了更早、更可靠的远距离检测，验证了密度感知融合和距离自适应机制对远距稀疏场景的有效性。

Abstract: 3D object detection is the backbone of perception for automated vehicles (AV) and broader intelligent transportation systems applications. Long-range detection is challenging because sensing evidence is sparse; yet this ``long-range'' scenario is routine in traffic. Although >30m is often labeled long-range in computer vision, on roadways it affords only approx. 1-2s for perception and decision-making. Under such extreme sparsity, two core challenges arise. First, early multimodal fusion tends to discard sparsity information and inject noise from empty or falsely occupied cells, degrading long-range recall. Second, context-agnostic uniform channel supervision favors dense and near-range samples, leaving far and small objects under-optimized, delaying the earliest detection of distant objects. We propose ``Ask The Neighbor'' (ATN3D), a LiDAR-Radar framework tailored for sparse-range conditions. ATN3D introduces (i) Density-aware early fusion with cross-modal gating that conditions fusion on per-voxel density/sparsity and Radar evidence, (ii) Occupancy-gated neighborhood aggregation with circular kernels to aggregate only from credible cells, (iii) Evidence-conditioned channel self-attention to adapt channel weights with weather/range, and (iv) a Range-aware loss that re-balances classification and localization by distance, aligning training with distance-stratified evaluation. On the VoD benchmark across clear and foggy conditions, ATN3D surpasses strong baselines: +3.55% mAP in clear weather and +8.41% mAP under simulated heavy fog; for >30m objects, gains are +3.33% (clear) and +2.09% (heavy fog). These results indicate earlier and more reliable long-range detections under sparse sensing in on-road traffic.

</details>


### [201] [CineDance: Towards Next-Generation Multi-Shot Long-Form Cinematic Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09639)
*Yuheng Chen,Teng Hu,Yuji Wang,Qingdong He,Zhucun Xue,Qianyu Zhou,Xiangtai Li,Lizhuang Ma,Jiangning Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CineDance-1M是一个大规模、开源的多镜头长视频文本-音频-视频（T2AV）数据集，通过三阶段策展流程和CineBench评估体系，旨在推动开放域长视频联合生成研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源视频生成模型受限于高质量训练数据的稀缺，而商业系统已展现生成电影级叙事的能力。为弥补这一差距，需要构建大规模、高质量的多模态长视频数据集。

Method: 构建CineDance-1M数据集，采用三阶段策展流程：多样化数据源与清洗、电影理论启发的叙事分割、层次化双模态标注。同时提出CineBench评估基准，包含多样提示集和六维人工对齐指标体系。基于LTX-2.0进行适配验证。

Result: 数据集平均时长92.8秒、连续镜头24.2个，提供可配置的结构化音频和视频标注；模型在单模态质量、音视频对齐、主体与环境一致性上均表现优异。

Conclusion: 该工作为多镜头长视频联合音频-视频生成研究提供了坚实的基础，有望加速该领域的未来发展。

Abstract: The fidelity and structural diversity of training datasets fundamentally determine the capabilities of video generation models. While commercial systems showremarkableabilitytogeneratecinematicnarratives, the progress of open-source models remains limited by the scarcity of high-quality training data. To bridge this gap, we introduce CineDance-1M, a large-scale, open research Text-to-Audio-Video (T2AV) dataset designed specifically for multi-shot, long-form joint audio-video generation. Averaging 92.8 seconds and 24.2 continuous shots per video, it provides configurable, structured annotations for both audio and video modalities. This exceptional quality is achieved through a rigorous three-stage curation pipeline: i) diverse sourcing and comprehensive cleansing, ii) film-theory-inspired narrative parsing, and iii) hierarchical dual-modal captioning. For a comprehensive assessment, we propose CineBench, featuring a diverse prompt suite and a six-dimensional, human-aligned metric system tailored for complex narrative audio-video evaluation. Furthermore, we adapt LTX-2.3 into CineDance, which demonstrates exceptional single-modality quality alongside precise audio-video alignment and robust subject and environment consistency, effectively validating our curation strategy and the high quality of CineDance-1M. We anticipate that this work will serve as a solid foundation for accelerating future research in multi-shot, long-form joint audio-video generation. Our project page is available at https://aliothchen.github.io/projects/CineDance/.

</details>


### [202] [MAVIS: Multi-Agent Video Retrieval via Structured Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09641)
*Jie Zhang,Qilang Ye,Hao Zhou,Haochen Liang,Fei Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAVIS是一种基于多智能体协作推理的视频检索框架，通过结构化语义库构建、子任务分解和逻辑感知辩论机制，替代传统全库扫描的嵌入检索范式，在无需微调的情况下达到竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于嵌入的全库扫描视频检索存在计算效率低下和语义不对称问题（视频信息密度高而文本查询稀疏），需要更高效的检索范式。

Method: 1) 将视频解析为结构化语义库（Structured Semantic Library），实现显式属性级索引；2) 规划器将复杂用户意图分解为原子子任务，分派专业智能体独立提名候选；3) 采用逻辑感知辩论机制（Logic-aware Debate）配合严格否决协议，智能体协作剪枝逻辑不匹配项，仅保留"有争议"候选进行细粒度验证。

Result: 在MSR-VTT、MSVD和ActivityNet上取得竞争力的性能，无需任务特定微调。

Conclusion: MAVIS提供了一种可扩展且可解释的替代方案，有效规避全库遍历的低效问题，将检索从暴力搜索重新定义为协作推理。

Abstract: The dominant paradigm in video retrieval relies on embedding-based full-corpus scanning, which suffers from inherent computational inefficiency and the semantic asymmetry between information-dense videos and sparse textual queries. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{MAVIS}, a novel multi-agent framework that rethinks retrieval as cooperative reasoning rather than brute-force search. MAVIS first bridges the granularity mismatch by parsing raw videos into a \textbf{Structured Semantic Library}, enabling explicit attribute-level indexing. During retrieval, a planner decomposes complex user intents into atomic sub-tasks, dispatching specialized agents to independently nominate candidates. Crucially, MAVIS employs a \textbf{Logic-aware Debate} mechanism with a strict veto protocol, where agents collaboratively prune logical mismatches to identify a compact set of ``controversial'' candidates for fine-grained verification. This agentic workflow effectively bypasses the inefficiency of full-library traversal. Extensive experiments on MSR-VTT, MSVD, and ActivityNet demonstrate that MAVIS achieves competitive performance without task-specific fine-tuning, offering a scalable and interpretable alternative to traditional dual-encoder approaches.

</details>


### [203] [Do Video Foundation Models Understand Intuitive Physics? A Layerwise Probing Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09646)
*Samuele Punzo,Niccolò Caselli,Ippokratis Pantelidis,Francesco Massafra,Salvatore Lo Sardo,Mohammadreza Salehi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了预训练视频基础模型在其冻结表征中是否编码了直观物理信息，并比较了V-JEPA、VideoMAE和LTX-Video三种模型在不同层和探针类型下的表现。V-JEPA整体最强，物理信息在中间至深层最易获取，时序破坏会显著降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练视频基础模型的冻结表征是否蕴含直观物理知识，以及这种知识在不同模型家族、网络层和探针类型之间的差异。

Method: 使用冻结特征探针（frozen-feature probing）在IntPhys2和Minimal Video Pairs（MVP）基准上进行评估，比较了联合嵌入预测模型（V-JEPA）、掩码重建模型（VideoMAE）和扩散视频生成模型（LTX-Video）的表现。

Result: V-JEPA在整体上表现最强，尤其在使用建模时序动态的探针时；VideoMAE保持竞争力；LTX-Video虽然较弱但仍有非平凡信号。物理相关信息在早期层最弱，在中间至深层最易获取。打乱帧顺序会显著降低性能，尤其是在MVP上。

Conclusion: 直观物理知识在预训练视频表征中可靠涌现，但其可获取性强烈依赖于预训练范式、表征深度和读出机制。

Abstract: We study whether pretrained video foundation models encode intuitive-physics information in their frozen representations, and how this information varies across model families, layers, and probe types. Using frozen-feature probing on IntPhys2 and Minimal Video Pairs (MVP), we compare predictive joint-embedding models (V-JEPA), masked reconstruction models (VideoMAE), and a diffusion-based video generator (LTX-Video). V-JEPA achieves the strongest overall results across benchmarks, especially with probes that model temporal dynamics, while VideoMAE remains competitive and LTX-Video recovers weaker but non-trivial signal. Layerwise analyses show that physics-relevant information is weakest in early layers and becomes most accessible at intermediate-to-late depth, and temporal controls show that disrupting frame order substantially reduces performance, especially on MVP. Together, these results suggest that intuitive-physics knowledge emerges reliably in pretrained video representations, but its accessibility depends strongly on pretraining paradigm, representational depth, and readout mechanism.

</details>


### [204] [Visual Prompting Meets Feature Reconstruction-Based Anomaly Detection with Dual-Teacher Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09670)
*Mateo Diaz-Bone,Daniel Caraballo,Florian Scheidegger,Thomas Frick,Mattia Rigotti,Andrea Bartezzaghi,Roy Assaf,Niccolo Avogaro,Yagmur G. Cinar,Brown Ebouky,Filip M. Janicki,Piotr S. Kluska,Cezary Skura,Cristiano Malossi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对异常检测方法在真实场景中因物体尺度、视角、背景、光照等变化导致性能下降的问题，提出了三项贡献：前景-背景掩码的视觉提示管道、解冻学生-教师模型中的教师以提升领域适应性、以及基于扩散生成图像的增强策略。在AeBAD数据集上，基于MMR骨干网络实现了3.5个百分点的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法在标准数据集（如MVTec）上表现完美，但当遇到物体尺度、视角、背景、光照和中心位置等基本假设被违反的真实场景时，这些方法往往失效，限制了其在实际应用中的可用性。

Method: (1) 基于前景-背景掩码的视觉提示管道，用于隔离目标物体；(2) 解冻学生-教师模型中的教师网络，以增强领域适应性；(3) 利用扩散模型生成合成图像进行数据增强。

Result: 在具有挑战性的AeBAD数据集上，以掩码多尺度重建（MMR）模型为骨干，取得了比先前最先进方法高3.5个百分点的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过视觉提示、教师解冻和扩散生成数据增强三项技术，有效提升了异常检测方法在真实场景变化下的鲁棒性和适应性。

Abstract: Recent Anomaly Detection methods achieve perfect detection and segmentation scores on well-established datasets, such as MVTec. However, many of these methods face challenges when foundational assumptions - such as consistent object scale, viewpoint, background, illumination, and centered placement - are violated. Those variations that occur render anomaly detection methods unusable in many real-world scenarios. To address these limitations, we introduce three key contributions: (1) a visual prompting pipeline that isolates objects using foreground-background masking; (2) a mechanism for unfreezing the teacher in student-teacher models to improve domain adaptability; and (3) a data augmentation strategy leveraging diffusion-generated synthetic images to enhance anomaly detection performance. We achieve a 3.5 percentage point improvement over the previous state-of-the-art on the challenging AeBAD dataset by using the Masked Multiscale Reconstruction (MMR) model as our backbone.

</details>


### [205] [SoccerNet 2026 Player-Centric Ball-Action Spotting:Retraining and Post-Processing Extensions to the FOOTPASS Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09679)
*Parthsarthi Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对SoccerNet 2026球员中心球动作定位挑战，在三个FOOTPASS基线系统基础上提出了四项改进：梯度检查点实现单GPU全骨干微调、GNNlogits融合到DST编码器、平方根频率类别加权处理数据不平衡、以及包含logit门控、时序帧优化、球衣重分配和双模型集成的后处理流程。在测试集和挑战集上分别达到0.548和0.446的Macro F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球广播视频中需要预测谁在何时执行何种动作，共8个动作类别，且存在严重的数据不平衡问题（传球与抢断比例213:1），现有基线系统性能有提升空间。

Method: 四项改进：(1)梯度检查点技术实现单GPU全骨干微调；(2)将GNNlogits融合到DST编码器中，结合图基战术上下文与逐球员视觉特征；(3)平方根频率类别加权处理213:1的传球-抢断不平衡；(4)后处理流水线包括每类logit门控、时序帧优化、球衣重分配和双模型集成。

Result: 测试集Macro F1为0.548，挑战集（服务器评估）为0.446。

Conclusion: 所提出的四项扩展有效提升了球员中心球动作定位的性能，特别是在处理数据不平衡和融合多模态特征方面取得了改进效果。

Abstract: We describe our system for the SoccerNet 2026 Player-Centric Ball-Action Spotting Challenge, which requires predicting who performs which action and when, across eight classes in broadcast soccer. Building on the three FOOTPASS baselines [1] (TAAD, TAAD+GNN, and TAAD+DST), we contribute four extensions: (1) gradient check pointing to enable full-backbone fine-tuning on a single GPU; (2) fusion of GNN logits into the DST encoder, combining graph-based tactical context with per-player visual features; (3) square-root frequency class weighting to address the 213:1 pass-to-tackle imbalance in the training data; and (4) a post processing pipeline comprising per-class logit gating, temporal frame refinement, jersey re-assignment, and a two-model ensemble. Our system achieves 0.548 Macro F1 on the test set and 0.446 on the challenge set (server evaluation).

</details>


### [206] [GenEyePose: Patient-Free, Knowledge-Based Saccadic Eye Movement Modeling for Digital Neurophysiologic Biomarker Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09681)
*Tianyu Lin,Jooyoung Ryu,Puvada Sreevarsha,Rahul Srinivasaragavan,Riya Satavlekar,Susan Kim,Nidhi Soley,Yujie Yan,Ishan Vatsaraj,Carl Harris,Aimon Rahman,Vishal Patel,Joseph Greenstein,Casey Taylor,Kemar E. Green*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个完全合成、无需真实患者的 multimodal 眼动生成流程，用于通用化扫视分析。利用合成数据训练的深度学习分类器在真实临床数据上区分正常与异常扫视（低/高测量）取得了 AUROC 0.76、灵敏度 0.71 的性能，证明合成数据在临床筛查和神经定位中有应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼动（尤其是扫视）是神经生理状态的敏感生物标志物，但现有 AI 视频眼动方案因隐私问题和数据集稀缺，缺乏用于筛查、分诊或定位脑部异常的鲁棒解决方案。

Method: 提出全合成、患者无关的多模态眼动生成流程，生成合成数据集；训练深度学习分类器区分正常与异常（低测量和超测量）扫视精度，并在真实临床数据上评估。

Result: 模型在真实临床数据上达到 AUROC 0.76、灵敏度 0.71，表明合成数据能泛化到临床应用。

Conclusion: 合成数据驱动的 AI 眼动分析可作为居家/急诊筛查工具或精确神经解剖定位手段，弥补传统脑成像的不足。

Abstract: Eye movements, including saccades, are widely regarded as highly sensitive and objective biomarkers of neurophysiologic states. Detecting saccadic signatures in neurologic diseases offers a rapid, portable alternative to brain imaging, avoiding access and cost barriers. Currently, there are no robust AI-enabled video-oculographic solutions (e.g., digital biomarkers) for screening, triaging, or localizing brain abnormalities due to privacy issues and scarce datasets. In this work, we propose the first fully synthetic, patient-free, multimodal eye movement generation pipeline for generalizable saccade analysis. Using this synthetic dataset, we trained a deep learning classifier to distinguish between normal and abnormal (hypometria and hypermetria) saccadic accuracies and evaluated its performance on real-world clinical data. The model achieved an AUROC of 0.76 and a sensitivity of 0.71, showing that the synthetic data has strong potential to generalize for clinical applications, including as a screening tool in at-home and emergency room settings or a tool for precise neuroanatomic localization.

</details>


### [207] [Cranio-Diff: Diffusion-based Cross-domain Craniofacial Reconstruction with 2D X-ray Skull Guidance and Structural Identity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09699)
*Ravi Shankar Prasad,Naresh Gurjar,Shashank Baghel,Chirag,Dinesh Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Cranio-Diff，一个基于扩散模型的跨域颅面重建框架，通过ControlNet的结构引导和生物特征文本条件，从2D X光颅骨图像生成语义和结构对齐的面部图像。在包含4320个配对样本的数据集上，该方法在图像质量和检索任务上均优于现有方法，可用于法医调查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（如CycleGAN、Pix2Pix、扩散模型）在人脸生成任务中表现优异，但在从颅骨（X光）到面部（光学）的跨模态颅面重建中，无法有效捕获跨模态语义信息，原因是结构身份对齐存在跨模态不匹配问题。

Method: 提出Cranio-Diff框架，基于扩散模型，通过ControlNet整合颅骨条件结构引导，并结合生物特征文本条件，生成与给定颅骨在语义和结构上更对齐的面部图像。

Result: 在120名受试者的正侧位X光颅骨-面部数据集上评估，每个面部图像跨三个年龄段（25、45、65）和三种BMI变化（-10%、基线、+10%）合成，共4320个配对样本。使用FID、IS、SSIM、LPIPS、PSNR和ArcFace分数评估图像质量，使用recall@k、mAP@k和MRR@k评估检索性能。实验结果表明该方法在生成图像质量和检索任务上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Cranio-Diff可作为法医调查中的辅助工具，为跨模态颅面重建提供有效支持。

Abstract: The state-of-the-art generative models, such as CycleGAN, Pix2Pix, and diffusion models have demonstrated remarkable performance in the face generation task. However, they fail to effectively capture cross-modality semantic information in craniofacial reconstruction when translating from the skull (x-ray) to the face (optical) domain, due to a mismatch in the alignment of structural identity across modalities. To address this issue, we propose Cranio-Diff, a diffusion-based framework for cross-domain cranio-facial reconstruction from 2D X-ray skull images. The proposed approach integrates skull-conditioned structural guidance through ControlNet with biometric text conditioning to generate a face which is more semantically and structurally aligned with the given skull. The proposed Cranio-diff method is evaluated on skull-face dataset obtained from X-ray scans of 120 subjects in lateral and frontal views. To enable controlled evaluation, each face image is synthesised across three age groups (25, 45, 65) and three BMI variations of -10%, baseline and +10%, yielding 4320 paired samples. To the best of our knowledge, this is the only X-ray-face dataset with this magnitude. Extensive experiments showed that the proposed method outperforms recent existing approaches in both generated image quality and retrieval task. Finally, to evaluate the performance of our proposed method, we have evaluated the quality of the generated image using FID, IS, SSIM, LPIPS, PSNR and ArcFace score. Additionally, retrieval performance is evaluated using recall@k, mAP@k and MRR@k. Obtained experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method can be used as an alternate tool in providing aid in forensic investigations.

</details>


### [208] [HDSL: A Hierarchical Domain-Specific Language for Structured 3D Indoor Scene Generation and Localized Editing with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09738)
*Letian Li,Chao Shen,Shuzhao Xie,Chenghao Gu,ZhengXiao He,Yu Meng,Xin Yang,Wenyuan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出层次化描述性场景语言(HDSL)，一种XML/CSS风格的结构化3D室内场景DSL，通过层次化程序生成与局部程序修复实现文本驱动的室内场景生成与编辑，相比基线方法在物体覆盖率、文本-场景对齐和编辑效率上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based室内场景系统依赖场景图或全局约束列表，这些中间表示紧凑但难以充分描述局部几何信息，且基于指令的编辑难以定位；需要一种语言模型既能生成又能修改的中间表示。

Method: 提出HDSL（XML/CSS风格DSL），将房间、区域、物体、支撑面表示为带局部坐标的树结构；使用LLM智能体通过有界验证生成HDSL子树，通过多模态资产检索对非虚拟节点进行接地，应用力导向布局优化修复边界和碰撞错误；编辑时通过层次化检索增强生成(HRAG)检索相关子树，仅重写局部上下文，经确定性三路合并回结果。

Result: 在重现基准中，HDSL在全场景文本-场景基线方法上提升了物体覆盖率和文本-场景对齐度，生成时间更短，同时在几何指标上与近期布局重现方法保持竞争力；HRAG减少5.22倍token使用和6.19倍运行时间，所有8个配对编辑均产生有效DSL，且更好地保留了无关场景物体。

Conclusion: HDSL通过层次化程序生成与局部程序修复框架，有效平衡了场景表示的完备性与编辑的可定位性，在生成质量和编辑效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Text-driven indoor scene generation and editing require an intermediate representation that language models can both produce and revise. Existing LLM-based systems often rely on scene graphs or global constraint lists, which are compact but underspecify local geometry and make instruction-based edits difficult to localize. We frame this problem as structured program generation and local program repair, and propose Hierarchical Descriptive Scene Language (HDSL), an XML/CSS-style domain-specific language for structured 3D indoor scenes. HDSL represents rooms, regions, objects, and support surfaces as a tree with local coordinates, making complex scenes easier to plan recursively and easier to retrieve for editing. Our pipeline uses LLM agents to generate HDSL subtrees with bounded verification, grounds non-virtual nodes through multimodal asset retrieval, and applies force-directed layout optimization to repair boundary and collision errors. For editing, Hierarchical Retrieval-Augmented Generation retrieves the relevant subtree, asks the LLM to rewrite only that local context, and merges the result back through a deterministic three-way merge. In our reproduced benchmark, HDSL improves average object coverage, text-scene alignment, and generation time over full text-to-scene baselines while remaining competitive with recent layout-only reproductions on geometry metrics; for editing, HRAG reduces token use by $5.22\times$ and runtime by $6.19\times$, produces valid DSL for all eight paired edits, and better preserves unrelated scene objects.

</details>


### [209] [Hybrid Robustness Verification for Spatio-Temporal Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09746)
*Sherwin Varghese,Matthew Wicker,Alessio Lomuscio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STBP框架，通过精确封闭形式刻画第一卷积层结合后续层可缩放近似，在3D CNN上实现更紧致的鲁棒性验证，相比现有方法提升1.7倍认证鲁棒准确率，并发布ST-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对3D CNN的鲁棒性验证方法要么使用过于保守的近似（如lp-norm扰动假设所有帧均被干扰），要么计算成本过高。实际中对抗扰动具有结构化的时空相关性，存在于低维语义子空间。需要一种基于现实时空约束的可扩展验证方法。

Method: 提出Spatio-Temporal Bound Propagation (STBP)框架：1) 对第一卷积层计算精确封闭形式解，提供最紧边界；2) 后续层使用可缩放近似传播认证边界；3) 将对抗扰动建模为时空约束（攻击者可修改子集帧或连续帧内的patch）。同时提出ST-Bench验证基准。

Result: 在UCF-101、Udacity、MedMNIST三个数据集上，STBP在相同扰动预算下比现有验证方法取得1.7倍更高的认证鲁棒准确率，同时显著提升可扩展性。

Conclusion: 建模现实时空约束能实现更紧致的近似，STBP在保证强鲁棒性认证的同时大幅提升可扩展性，ST-Bench可推动该领域系统评估。

Abstract: With AI increasingly deployed in safety-critical systems, providing formal robustness guarantees for the underlying models is essential. Existing verification methods either rely on overly conservative approximations or incur prohibitive computational costs. For example, the use of lp-norm perturbations in video settings encodes the belief that the adversary can inject noise in every video frame. In practice, adversarial perturbations exhibit structured spatial and temporal correlations, constrained to lower-dimensional, semantically meaningful subspaces. In this work, we study robustness verification of 3D CNNs processing video and volumetric inputs, targeting applications in action recognition (UCF-101), autonomous driving (Udacity), and medical imaging (MedMNIST) exploiting realistic assumptions on adversarial strength by modelling them as spatio-temporal constraints - where the attacker can modify either a subset of frames or patches within a set of consecutive frames. We demonstrate that modelling realistic constraints enables tighter approximations. We introduce Spatio-Temporal Bound Propagation (STBP), a verification framework that computes an exact closed-form characterization of the first convolutional layer and propagates certified bounds through subsequent layers using scalable approximations. Computing the exact closed form provides the tightest bounds for the first convolutional layer. Thus, we utilise approximation methods in the remainder of the network. To spur further progress in this field, we propose ST-Bench, a verification benchmark for autonomous driving and activity recognition, to systematically evaluate verifiable robustness. Compared to existing verification-based approaches, STBP provides stronger robustness guarantees with significantly improved scalability, achieving 1.7x higher certified robust accuracy under identical perturbation budgets.

</details>


### [210] [SemDINO: A DINOv3-Driven Network for Cross-Temporal Semantic Alignment in Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09772)
*Xinyu Tong,Meihua Zhou,Jinxiao Sun,Yingjie Tang,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SemDINO，一种端到端语义变化检测网络，通过双分支编码器（CNN+冻结DINOv3）、多尺度时序交互、语义净化与变化增强模块，在复杂干扰场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义变化检测方法存在跨时序对齐不足、多尺度表示弱、对光照/季节/配准噪声等伪变化鲁棒性差的问题。

Method: SemDINO包含：(1) 双分支编码器（CNN骨干+冻结DINOv3特征通过门控金字塔融合）；(2) 多尺度时序双向Transformer交互模块(M-TBTT)实现全局跨时序对齐；(3) 语义净化(SCP)、双向变化增强(BiChangeEnhance)和多尺度变化增强(MCE)协同抑制伪变化；(4) 多分支CD预测头联合输出二值变化掩码、双时相语义图及边缘约束。

Result: 在公开遥感CD数据集上的大量实验表明，SemDINO在复杂干扰场景下性能显著优于SOTA方法，且泛化能力强。

Conclusion: SemDINO通过融合CNN与Vision Transformer特征、多尺度时序交互及变化增强机制，有效提升了语义变化检测在复杂场景下的准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Semantic change detection (SCD) aims to simultaneously locate land-cover changes and identify semantic categories before and after transition. However, existing methods suffer from insufficient cross-temporal alignment, weak multi-scale representation, and poor robustness to pseudo-changes caused by illumination, season, and registration noise. To address these issues, we propose a novel end-to-end semantic change detection network named SemDINO, which integrates a dual-branch encoder, multi-scale temporal interaction, semantic purification, change enhancement, and decoupled multi-task prediction into a unified framework. Specifically, we construct a dual-branch encoder that combines a CNN backbone and frozen DINOv3 features via gated pyramid fusion, enabling rich multi-scale semantic representation. Then, a multi-scale temporal bidirectional transformer interaction (M-TBTT) module is proposed to achieve global cross-temporal feature alignment and information interaction. To further enhance genuine changes and suppress pseudo-variations, we introduce semantic purification (SCP), bidirectional change enhancement (BiChangeEnhance), and multi-scale change enhancement (MCE) modules collaboratively. Finally, a multi-branch CD prediction head is designed to jointly output binary change mask, bi-temporal semantic maps, and edge constraint. Extensive experiments on public remote sensing CD datasets demonstrate that SemDINO achieves superior performance and generalization ability against state-of-the-art methods, especially in complex scenarios with interference factors.

</details>


### [211] [POTATR: A Lightweight Image-to-Graph Model for Page-Level Table Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09788)
*Brandon Smock,Libin Liang,Max Sokolov,Amrit Ramesh,Valerie Faucon-Morin,Tayyibah Khanam,Maury Courtland*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: POTATR是一个仅29M参数的轻量级图像到图模型，在页面级表格提取任务上以极低成本和极快速度超越了包括前沿多模态大模型在内的所有模型，GriTS_Con达到0.964，速度提升130倍以上，成本降低约300倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格提取方法要么需要数十亿参数和大量自回归步骤导致效率低下，要么依赖昂贵的API推理。大规模文档处理需要既准确又高效的上下文感知表格提取方法。

Method: 提出POTATR，一个29M参数的图像到图模型，扩展了Table Transformer (TATR)进行上下文化的页面级表格提取。输出每个元素都带有边界框，实现空间对齐，支持视觉验证和几何文本分配。

Result: 在PubTables-v2 Single Pages基准上达到GriTS_Con 0.964，超越所有测试模型（包括前沿多模态大模型），运行速度提升130倍以上，成本降低约300倍。

Conclusion: POTATR实现了统一的页面级表格提取，可与其他模型组合使用，通过外部OCR扩展到扫描文档，通过跨页合并等技术扩展到全文档表格提取，代码和模型将开源。

Abstract: Large-scale document processing requires contextually aware table extraction (TE) that is both accurate and efficient. Yet current approaches require billions of parameters, hundreds of autoregressive steps, or costly API inference. Motivated by this, we introduce the Page-Object Table Transformer (POTATR), a lightweight 29M parameter image-to-graph model that extends the Table Transformer (TATR) for contextualized page-level TE. POTATR outperforms all models tested on the PubTables-v2 Single Pages benchmark -- including frontier MLLMs -- achieving $\textrm{GriTS}_\textrm{Con}$ of 0.964 while running over 130$\times$ faster at roughly 300$\times$ lower cost. Further, POTATR's output is spatially grounded: every recognized element has a bounding box, enabling visual verification and geometric text assignment. As a result, POTATR performs unified page-level TE while composing with other models, enabling extension to scanned documents via external OCR and to full-document TE via techniques like cross-page merging. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [212] [End-to-End Optimization of Incoherent Imaging for Classification Under Detector-Limited Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09792)
*Archer Wang,Joshua Chen,Sachin Vaidya,Marin Soljačić*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了非相干成像中相位掩模与神经网络后端端到端联合优化的优势条件，证明在完全探测器读出下，传统聚焦透镜接近理论性能上限，联合优化无增益；而在受限读出下（粗空间采样或有限测量数），优化光学系统可通过提高类别可分性显著提升分类性能，增益在低噪声时最大并随噪声增大而减小，且依赖于任务的频谱结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管端到端联合优化光学前端（如超表面）与神经网络后端已被广泛应用于成像任务，但缺乏一个形式化的理论框架来刻画这种系统何时以及为何优于传统透镜成像。

Method: 通过理论分析与数学证明，结合合成数据和标准基准（MNIST、FashionMNIST、SVHN）上的实验验证，系统研究相位掩模联合优化在不同探测器读出条件下的性能。

Result: 完全探测器读出下，无任何非相干相位掩模能超越理想信道互信息上限，传统聚焦透镜接近该上限；受限探测器读出下，优化光学可显著提升分类性能，增益在低噪声时最大，且当类别判别信息集中在低于类内变化的低频空间频率时优势最明显。

Conclusion: 本研究首次为光学-神经网络联合优化的适用条件提供了严格的理论框架，阐明了探测器读出约束和噪声水平是决定联合优化收益的关键因素。

Abstract: End-to-end co-optimization of optical front-ends (e.g. metasurfaces) and neural network back-ends has been widely applied to imaging tasks, yet a formalism characterizing when and why such systems outperform conventional lens-based imaging is largely lacking. This paper focuses on object classification, a central imaging task, and asks when end-to-end optimization of a phase mask for incoherent imaging improves performance over a conventional focusing lens. We find that these gains arise primarily under constrained detector readout and are limited under full detector readout. In the latter setting, we prove that no incoherent phase mask exceeds the ideal-channel mutual information between detector measurements and class labels; a conventional focusing lens approaches this ceiling, and joint optimization yields no empirical gain. When detector readout is constrained -- by coarse spatial sampling or a limited number of measurements -- optimized optics can substantially improve classification by increasing class separability in the detector measurements. These gains are largest under low detector noise and shrink as noise grows, because the optics shape the signal before it reaches the detector but cannot remove noise added afterward. The advantage also depends on the spectral structure of the task: co-design helps most when class-discriminative content is concentrated at lower spatial frequencies than within-class variation. We develop a theoretical framework formalizing these distinctions and test its predictions on synthetic data and standard benchmarks (MNIST, FashionMNIST, SVHN).

</details>


### [213] [Beyond Spherical Harmonics: Rethinking Appearance Models for Radiance Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09794)
*Ewa Miazga,Jorge Condor,Piotr Didyk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种球面函数在场景重建中的表现，提出了一种新型归一化各向异性球面Gabor函数，能够在保持紧凑表示的同时高效建模和学习高频视角相关外观效果，相比现有方法内存效率提升5倍，在辐射场重建任务中实现了更高质量的视角依赖现象重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于球谐函数（SH）的方法在处理视角相关外观时，高频效果（如镜面反射）需要高阶展开，导致内存和计算开销大；而低阶SH又无法捕捉复杂视角依赖效应，导致表示过于平滑或漫反射化。因此需要一种既能高效表示高频外观、又保持紧凑性的球面函数。

Method: 系统评估了多种球面函数在场景重建中的表现，其中部分函数为本文首次引入图形学和计算机视觉领域。基于实验洞察，提出归一化各向异性球面Gabor函数（Normalized Anisotropic Spherical Gabor function），实现了高频外观效应的高效建模与学习，同时保持紧凑表示。

Result: 相比现有方法，所提函数在视角依赖现象（如闪烁/glints）的重建质量更高，内存效率提升达5倍，且计算效率更优。

Conclusion: 在辐射场重建任务中验证了该方法的有效性，表明归一化各向异性球面Gabor函数能够有效替代传统SH等方法，在内存和计算效率上取得显著提升的同时保持高质量的重建效果。

Abstract: View-dependent appearance modeling remains a challenging problem in novel-view synthesis and reconstruction. Accurately representing complex angular effects often requires substantial memory and computational resources. For new learning-based methods, a common approach is to rely on SH. However, capturing high-frequency phenomena such as specular reflections demands high-order expansions, which increase memory usage and computational cost. Consequently, most methods employ low-order SH, which limits the ability to model complex view-dependent effects, resulting in overly smooth or diffuse representations. To address these limitations, we systematically evaluate a wide range of spherical functions in the context of scene reconstruction. Some of them are introduced to graphics and computer vision for the first time in this paper. Based on the insights from the experiment, we develop a novel spherical formulation, the Normalized Anisotropic Spherical Gabor function that enables efficient modeling and learning of high-frequency appearance effects while maintaining compact representation. Compared to existing approaches, our function achieves higher-quality reconstruction of view-dependent phenomena such as glints, while being up to five times more memory-efficient and more efficient to evaluate. We validate its performance in radiance-field reconstruction tasks.

</details>


### [214] [Echo-Memory: A Controlled Study of Memory in Action World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09803)
*Wayne King,Zeyue Xue,Yuxuan Bian,Jie Huang,Haoran Li,Yaowei Li,Yaofeng Su,Yuming Li,Haoyu Wang,Shiyi Zhang,Songchun Zhang,Yuwei Niu,Sihan Xu,Junhao Zhuang,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Echo-Memory，在控制变量的条件下系统研究了动作条件世界模型中的记忆机制。通过固定视频扩散骨干网络、优化器、动作表示等所有其他组件，仅改变历史信息的存储和读取方式，对比了原始上下文、压缩记忆、空间摘要和状态空间循环等四种记忆设计。使用三分支评估协议（回放质量、域内循环访问、开放域返回探测）发现：回放保真度不能充分代表世界记忆能力；原始上下文在开放域返回任务中表现突出；激进压缩会丢失关键证据；块状状态空间循环是最强的开放域返回机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型在记忆机制方面存在失败（相机离开再返回时场景或物体可能悄然改变），但不同记忆设计之间的比较因骨干网络、训练、检索和评估差异而难以进行公平对比，需要解耦记忆与其他因素进行系统研究。

Method: 固定视频扩散骨干网络、优化器、相机动作表示、采样器和评估管线，仅改变历史信息的存储和读取方式，构建了四种记忆设计（原始上下文、压缩记忆、空间摘要、状态空间循环）的匹配矩阵，并通过三分支评估协议（回放质量、域内循环访问、开放域返回探测）进行多维度评估。

Result: 原始上下文是强容量基线，在开放域返回任务上远超回放指标；激进压缩会丢失关键证据；块状状态空间循环是最强的开放域返回机制；回放保真度不能充分代理世界记忆能力。

Conclusion: Echo-Memory提供了一个紧凑的协议用于研究动作世界模型中的记忆，超越了孤立的回放指标，揭示了隐式记忆的结构与是否使用记忆同样重要。

Abstract: We present \textbf{Echo-Memory}, a controlled study of memory mechanisms in action-conditioned world models. These models generate multi-segment videos from a first frame, text prompt, and camera-action sequence, but their central failure is often memory rather than local image synthesis: after the camera leaves and returns, the scene or salient object may silently change. Existing memory designs are hard to compare because gains are entangled with backbone, training, retrieval, and evaluation differences. Echo-Memory fixes the action-to-video interface and varies only how history is stored and read by the generator. Under a shared video diffusion backbone, optimizer, camera-action representation, sampler, and evaluation pipeline, we compare raw context, compression-based memory, spatial summaries with different read-out paths, and state-space recurrence. This matched matrix separates four otherwise conflated axes: \emph{capacity}, \emph{compression}, \emph{read-out}, and \emph{recurrence}. We also evaluate memory through a three-branch protocol: replay quality, in-domain loop revisit, and open-domain return probes. The branches routinely disagree, showing that replay fidelity is not a sufficient proxy for remembering a world. Three findings follow. Raw context is a strong capacity baseline and improves open-domain return far more than it improves replay metrics. Compactness is not a free substitute for capacity: aggressive spatial and hybrid-compression memories lose the salient evidence needed for return. Finally, block-wise state-space recurrence is the strongest open-domain return mechanism in our matrix, showing that the structure of implicit memory matters as much as the decision to use it. These results provide a compact protocol for studying memory in action world models beyond isolated replay metrics.

</details>


### [215] [PTL-Diffusion: Manifold-Aware Diffusion with Periodic Terminal Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09816)
*Danqi Zhuang,Jisui Huang,Xiaoyue Xi,Andrew Kiggins,Xiaojie Wang,Ke Chen,Yue Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PTL-Diffusion框架，将扩散模型的终端分布从单一高斯分布改为非恒定的周期高斯分布族，在正向噪声过程中嵌入相位结构，在流形级分布匹配上优于标准DDPM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型使用单一时间齐次的高斯终端分布作为生成参考律，这种无结构的选择导致数据集中在低维流形上时，逆向模型必须几乎完全从无结构的终端参考分布中恢复流形级结构，缺乏显式的结构引导。

Method: 提出PTL-Diffusion框架：1) 使用周期受力的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck型正向过程，收敛到非恒定的周期高斯终端分布族而非单一不变分布；2) 推导了闭合形式的正向边缘分布、极限周期高斯终端族和显式高斯逆向后验，支持标准噪声预测训练；3) 引入不变平均正则化项，通过平均周期参考分布耦合相位条件逆向动力学。

Result: 在环面和圆柱点云基准以及Olivetti人脸数据集上，PTL-Diffusion在流形级分布匹配上优于匹配的DDPM基线，减少了相位条件误差、特征空间协方差误差和最近邻流形距离。

Conclusion: 结构化的终端参考律是扩散模型有前途的发展方向，需要更富表达力的相位构造和更大规模的评估。

Abstract: Standard diffusion models typically use a single time-homogeneous Gaussian terminal distribution as the reference law for generation. While this choice is analytically convenient and empirically powerful, it provides little explicit structure for data concentrated near low-dimensional manifolds, where different regions of the data distribution may correspond to distinct local geometric or semantic factors. As a result, the reverse model must recover manifold-level structure almost entirely from an unstructured terminal reference distribution.
